# Evidence index: the `evidence` bucket

Every object in the private Supabase storage bucket `evidence` (project `htdblwldjkcgjvmqkbeh`), uploaded 26 Oct 2026 by the ceo seat from the laptop's `Desktop` folders. See `README.md` in this folder for why these files are not in git. The sha256 values match the laptop originals.

## How it was verified (26 Oct 2026)

- **Count and bytes:** the bucket listing (`storage.objects`) holds 2498 objects. Each object's listed size equals the local file's size, and the total is 1,950,833,406 bytes.
- **Content:** every object was downloaded back with the service role and hashed: 2498 of 2498 match the local file's sha256 and size.
- **Access:** reads without the service role were refused:
  - anon key, authenticated path: HTTP 400. Refused: true.
  - anon key, plain object path: HTTP 400. Refused: true.
  - no key: HTTP 400. Refused: true.
  - public URL: HTTP 400. Refused: true.
  - anon list of the bucket: HTTP 200, 0 items listed. Refused: true.

| why | objects | bytes |
|---|---:|---:|
| third-party | 322 | 692,120,713 |
| binary | 2098 | 717,194,842 |
| over-5MB | 78 | 541,517,851 |
| **total** | **2498** | **1,950,833,406** |

The `why` values:

- **binary**: a screenshot, recording, PDF or office file of 5 MB or less, kept out of git by decision.
- **over-5MB**: anything over 5,000,000 bytes.
- **third-party**: real companies' or people's data. It never comes into git.

Object keys are the original path with every character outside `A-Z a-z 0-9 . _ / -` replaced by `_`. The original Desktop path is shown only where it differs from the key.

## Added 27 Oct 2026: source packs from the laptop project folder

Three folders sat in the laptop checkout, ignored by git and in no bucket. They were uploaded by the ceo seat under the prefix `laptop_project_folder/` before the laptop was cleared:

- the founder's knowledge pack, `knowledge-import/all86_full` (86 categories, v2026.07.31). The smaller `all86` copy holds no file that this one lacks.
- the Phase D spec package (v2026.08.17).
- `e2e-reports`.

**Verified:** 287 objects and 38,004,550 bytes. All 287 were downloaded back with the service role and match the local sha256 and size. A read without a key returned HTTP 400, so it was refused.

## Added 27 Oct 2026: the founder's QuotesDone files from the laptop home folder

Before the laptop was cleared, the ceo seat uploaded these under the prefix `laptop_home/`:

- the QuotesDone documents in `Downloads`: knowledge packs, phase packages, the investor pack, assessments, briefs, the supplier list and the DMARC report.
- `qd_packs`, which holds the unzipped autonomy and upgrade packs.
- `quotesdone-wip-backups`.
- `pr689_review`.
